# PRICE ACTION RESEARCH REPORT

LIVE = OFF. SCALPING LIVE = OFF. PRICE ACTION LIVE = OFF. Isolated = enforced. Risk Engine = authoritative. No VALIDATED_FOR_PAPER. No future leakage.

Pattern statistics are forward labels. They are not live features and are not strategy PnL.
Cup & Handle = NOT_IMPLEMENTED (no objective causal detector).
Multiple-testing / selection bias: many patterns and variants were scored on the same bars. IS_PROMISING is not validation.

1m/3m were ingested via existing `ResearchKlineCache` (fapi klines). Taker/OI/funding/basis were not fabricated. TONUSDT taker coverage is 0% so taker features stay DATA_UNAVAILABLE on that coin.

## Hypotheses tested

Count: 14
- sequence lengths 2-6+ bullish/bearish independently
- bullish sequence then rejection
- bearish sequence then rejection
- compression then expansion
- expansion continuation vs exhaustion
- W detected vs W + neckline confirmation (separate)
- M detected vs M + neckline confirmation (separate)
- flag/pennant/triangle/wedge/rectangle breakout direction measured
- liquidity sweep rejection
- breakout + retest vs failed breakout
- H&S / inverse H&S neckline close
- structure BOS
- Model B IS/VAL/OOS + cost BASE/1.25/1.5/2.0
- occupancy same-coin collision
